Evaluate Lily's preferred On Me flow early after processor/privacy and budget controls; test her real outfits, shoes, layered swaps, source-faithful/representative distinctions, 1–3-image readiness and historical search. Find One needs its real web-source/privacy/cost/quality and purchase/reminder gates; enable neither from a demo alone. If image quality falls short, review the scope with Lily rather than claim board-only equivalence. Verify purchased wallet recovery before packs.
5. Run core and all applicable enabled-feature evidence checks on physical iPhone/iPad devices. Record unresolved findings and actual build evidence; recheck current Apple rules immediately before submission.
6. Before public launch, publish the compact Section 22.3 product/support website, essential contextual help and versioned release notes. Grow guides/tools/comparisons from repeated needs; add the independently optional early-user welcome/check-in program only after its communication controls are ready.

### 26.5 PRD v2 revision record — October 3, 2026

This revision consolidates Lily's supplied interview with the owner's accepted clarification: petite means demonstrated fit for her proportions; shopping should offer the closest evidence-supported garment/shoe choices with her freedom to refine; visual previews should resemble actual selected pieces as closely as validated processing permits; and delivered previews should remain available for later search and reuse.

- Corrected measurement provenance: confirmed height 4′11″; inseam unknown until Lily confirms it. Fit rules use confirmed measurements, garment evidence and her feedback rather than a Petite label or weight-based sizing.
- Replaced a mandatory Risky lane with Safe and Elevated owned directions; shopping is explicit. Target three directions, allowing independently verified one/two-look wardrobe partials without inventing inventory or forcing a purchase.
- Made opt-in On Me Lily's preferred initial visual experience, with separate fidelity, supported-piece, privacy, cost and provider-lifecycle gates. Boards remain useful independently. No image provider or complete-outfit accuracy is assumed.
- Added automatically retained preview history, immutable composition/source versions and honest quality labels. Favorites and collections organize history; ordinary edits, reference replacement and subscription expiry do not delete it. Reviewed privacy/user deletion still removes its disclosed scope.
- Added unified free/offline search across garments, associated images, outfits and retained previews: editable keywords, phrase/prefix/alias matching, facets and source/version labels. Pixel-based similarity remains a later gated enhancement; metadata search does not claim to understand untagged image pixels.
- Integrated today's reviewed clothing context, color scope, real-outfit-photo ownership review, named collections, closer shopping matches and closet versatility evidence. Explicit Update Preview, multiple collection membership and finite per-child image attempts are proposed implementation defaults, subject to prototype validation.
- Preserved v0.1 and Lily's source feedback; updated the README and companion pointers to this master. Structural document checks do not substitute for the release checks, which remain Not run.
- Added the confirmed mock-development requirement: real screens and durable workflows can be completed with fictional data and simulated text/image services before production provider selection, while live image feasibility is tested early in parallel.
- Confirmed local structured styling history and reuse before paid inference, including six-month revisits, current-context revalidation, explicit New ideas, exact retained-output reuse and billing only for admitted new image work. Added dependency/retention/export rules and mock-clock release checks; raw-conversation/sensitive-text retention and a long-lived backend closet replica are not part of this decision.
- Confirmed plain-language garment names, additive personal aliases/details, labeled possible matches and an explicit Remember this name correction loop, with current-ID disambiguation for styling. Naming/search stays local and free, preserves originals/history/render identity, and never silently learns from clicks. Optional photo understanding has a separate review/permission/adapter capability and real-evidence gate; it is not automatic photo access for the existing text stylist or try-on provider.
- Confirmed visible Dirty search/picker/history badges and individual/selected/confirmed-all laundry updates, restricted to the reviewed still-owned Dirty set with durable counts, safe undo and revision/sync conflict checks. Added No longer own as retained historical ownership distinct from Archive/deletion; only explicit reownership restores current ownership and still requires availability review. No status-only operation regenerates previews or spends AI units. Existing private iCloud/export/new-phone recovery and years-long retention requirements remain in Sections 15.1–15.3; automatic cloud-only offloading remains unselected.
- Confirmed required V1 Suitcases: multiple named canonical garment subsets, shared multi-membership without duplication, latest selected source remembered across launches and editable in Settings, Main Closet covering all clothing, and strict suitcase styling with an explicit Main Closet switch. Integrated scope into lookup/requests/reuse, shared status and scoped laundry, safe container lifecycle, private sync/export and AC-91–95/RV-27; no implementation or live platform validation is claimed.

### 26.6 Suitcase integration audit — October 4, 2026

Reviewed the Suitcases integration across the master and companion pointers, including three independent read-only reviews of storage/recovery, search/history and AI/cost behavior. The confirmed product choices remain unchanged. Corrected these documentation gaps:

- Add garments now has an explicit Main Closet management picker, with reviewed canonical links and no source switch, duplicate records, ownership inference or AI request.
- Saved source filtering uses captured provenance rather than current membership. Manual outfits, drafts, snapshots and previews retain that provenance independently of AI; removed container labels become Former suitcase, and unknown legacy sources stay Unknown.
- Working draft/action source is distinct from the remembered view. Automatic fallback gives browsing access; a deliberate source choice is required before broader styling. Incomplete recovered membership is Loading rather than verified empty, and source-wide absence/counts require readiness evidence. Pending image bytes do not by themselves block independent text/manual use.
- Generation and swaps share the scoped request contract. Image parent/child/history records capture scope/context outside exact render identity; current application is fenced to the target draft. Already-dispatched permitted outputs can finish into Earlier history. Typed idempotency context prevents reuse across different sources without storing a surrogate hash of sensitive prose.
- Scoped laundry checks reviewed membership/removal and container state as well as ownership/availability revisions, including remove/re-add races. Shopping absence wording and compatibility/count records now carry the applicable source.

Expanded existing AC-76/91–95, RV-23/24/27 and TO-06 in this Suitcases audit rather than adding unrelated features. Subsequent coverage changes are recorded in Sections 26.7/26.8; current counts are in Section 26.1. Document structure/source-preservation checks are separate from application evidence. All app/platform checks remain **Not run**.

Before release, prove atomic membership/laundry writes, current versus captured-source indexing, inventory-readiness and two-device recovery, stale-action/idempotency fences, zero-call/zero-unit local reuse and admitted-miss image accounting through real application paths. Optional private CloudKit recovery needs actual fresh-device evidence; enabled personal images require the applicable TO gates. No additional product decision is needed for these corrections, and this audit does not establish that an implementation is bug-free.


### 26.7 Measurement-aware stylist and web-search shopping revision — October 4, 2026

The owner accepted the simpler AI web-search/link finder and requested stronger measurement-aware styling plus robustness verification. Three independent read-only reviews examined shopping/spec conflicts, measurement prompting and search/privacy/cost orchestration. Primary OpenAI search/structured-output/safety documentation and Apple notification guidance were checked; no live API, app, purchase, sync or device test was run.

- Replaced the first-release catalog/fit-vendor prerequisite with genuine bounded search, source-bearing retailer links and isolated tool-free personal ranking. Specialized providers remain optional future adapters. Updated product flow, FR-12, architecture/stack, images/rights, entities/privacy, phases, risks and dependent acceptance/release references; no former catalog prerequisite remains current.
- Added a concrete provider-neutral stylist-policy/1 and minimal typed measurement/context rules in Section 8.5. Confirmed height, unknown inseam, fit-feedback priority, independent measurement basis, coordinated colors, strict source/ID/status guards, controlled swaps and model/prompt rollback are explicit. A prompt cannot manufacture fit evidence or guarantee physical results.
- Specified source/claim/variant validation, visible citations/unknowns, safe URLs, injection resistance, separate public/private requests, compatible backend controls, finite full tool/model cost admission and stale-context recovery. Configured bounds still need live provider proof; mock behavior is not API-capability or cost evidence.
- Added optional private arrival/photo/description completion reminders and explicit purchase/arrival state, with text-only saving, zero-call local actions, conservative not-arrived exclusion even through overrides/laundry, durable restart/sync/export and device-local notification reconciliation. No automatic suitcase/outfit addition, retailer-account access, checkout or cloud push service is introduced.
- Added FR-69–71, AC-96–101 and RV-28/29; strengthened existing AC-9/50–53 and RV-21. At completion of this revision, coverage was 71 functional requirements, 101 acceptance criteria, 29 RV checks and eight conditional TO checks; the latest counts are in Section 26.1. Original source artifacts remain preserved.

Before release, prove hard constraint/evidence/privacy guards, actual Lily styling/search usefulness, enforceable live cost/deadline behavior and browser/notification/device recovery. Existing core/Suitcase and conditional image gates still apply. All application acceptance/RV/TO checks remain **Not run**. This revision does not select a production search/text/image provider or certify any implementation.


### 26.8 Fit-first shopping and preferred-source clarification — October 4, 2026

The owner made size and fit the primary shopping value and authorized prioritizing Amazon, TikTok Shop, Target, Walmart, H&M and other retailers. This refines the accepted web-search route; it does not select retailer APIs or guarantee live chart/variant access. A read-only independent review checked fit gates, retailer preferences, measurement privacy and finite-search behavior; representative primary size-chart documentation was checked.

- Added Section 13.8's non-compensatory fit eligibility/ranking: SupportedGuidance, NeedsFitConfirmation and KnownFitConflict; product/seller/variant/chart applicability; relevant critical criteria with confirmed provenance; no physical-fit or fabricated confidence claim. Unknown Lily inseam stays unknown.
- Updated the public search and private stylist prompts, profile/request/result/assessment records, screen/product flow and old no-priority language. Preferred sources are editable discovery biases, with optional reviewed order and explicit retailer-only mode; fit evidence wins and other hard constraints remain mandatory. Five sources never authorize five independent search budgets or claim exhaustive coverage.
- Preserved private body context, allowed public garment-criterion review, user choice, scoped wardrobe/purchase/arrival/reminder behavior and image rights. Physical fit feedback is optional and explicit; generated images, saving, clicking and purchase are not fit proof.
- Added FR-72 and AC-102, strengthened AC-51/96/98/101 and RV-28/29, and aligned companion pointers. Current coverage is 72 functional requirements, 102 acceptance criteria, 29 RV checks and eight conditional TO checks.

Document checks do not establish model or retailer behavior. All application/RV/TO tests remain **Not run**. Before enabling these sources, prove actual permitted access, applicable charts/variants, deterministic fit exclusions/unknowns, bounded costs, real Lily outcomes and current retailer handoff on physical devices. Original source artifacts remain unchanged.

### 26.9 Optional ChatGPT plan connection research — October 4, 2026

The owner requested OpenAI eligibility/native technical validation and a current official Apple guideline check. Two read-only independent reviews examined provider access/auth/control differences and Apple login/privacy/payment/review requirements. Section 24.5 records current primary sources, commercial/native/backend unknowns, preview limitations and conditional prototype evidence. This is research, not authorization to add a working feature, alter public pricing, make ChatGPT login mandatory or replace the current funded route. No forms were submitted, accounts connected, device/API tests run or approval received. Existing counts remain 72 functional requirements, 102 acceptance criteria, 29 RV checks and eight conditional TO checks, all application checks **Not run**.

### 26.10 First-use quick tours approved for the real app — October 7, 2026

Jose explicitly approved adding the completed screen-tour design to this master PRD. Added FR-73, Section 7.8, TourProgress / TourPreferences and AC-103; updated onboarding, Settings/help and document navigation/counts. Coverage now includes every shipped user-facing screen, with shared logical IDs across responsive layouts. The earlier [first-use tours amendment](PRD_Amendment_First_Use_Tours.md) is accepted and superseded by this integrated specification, including the expanded screen coverage.

The Home and 37 additional HTML tour mockups, Profile replay links and shared review site are complete; all 102 additional HTML tips were rendered and checked. Those checks establish mockup layout/interaction only. Native automatic first-use tracking, preferences, persistence, accessibility and highlighted-control activation remain to implement and test. All application acceptance/RV/TO checks remain **Not run**. This decision approves tours, not unrelated mockup proposals, production services or purchases. No commit was made.
